an admirable quality."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A particular manifestation of moral excellence in a person."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Better to be excluded from society than to deny the virtue of citizenship.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Specifically, each of several qualities held to be particularly important, including the four cardinal virtues, the three theological virtues, or the seven virtues opposed to the seven deadly sins."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An inherently advantageous or excellent quality of something or someone;
